Let's design functions:

- feasible_project(h, target): clip, adjust sum. For large n, efficient. We need exact sum? Floating sum may not be exactly target; final maybe. We can adjust last not saturated. Projection algorithm:
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=n/2
   For up to 20 iterations:
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      # adjust free variables (0<h<1)
      free = (h>1e-8)&(h<1-1e-8)
      if free.any():
          h[free]+=diff/free.count()
      else:
          # impossible? distribute all
          h += diff/n
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # final exact? If due to floating residual, adjust one free variable by residual
   res=target-h.sum(); if free: find index to adjust by res within bounds.
   return h.
- evaluate_c5(h): dx=2/n; corr=np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full'); return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
- evaluate_corr(h): return corr maybe.

Smoothing/weights:
- compute_weights(q, tau, top=None): qmax=np.max(q); stable=(q-qmax)/tau; max stable 0; if tau very small, many -inf. To avoid underflow, we can select top M = min(len, max(1, int(50))) with weights softmax over top. Simpler: sort indices descending? Sorting 319 each iter okay but in Python sort maybe.    weights_all softmax with tau for objective/grad; if fewer than 2 nonzero, add small uniform over top T.
   We can compute raw = (q - qmax)/tau; raw = np.clip(raw, -700, 0); exps=np.exp(raw); w=exps/exps.sum(). If sum=0 (underflow due all -? qmax should give exp0=1) so sum≥1.
- grad_lags(h, weights, n): weights length 2n-1 over output indices. Need compute gradient of C = sum weights*q = dx*sum weights*corr. We can ignore dx during accumulation and multiply.
   offsets = np.arange(-(n-1), n)
   grad = zeros(n)
   indices = np.nonzero(weights > threshold)[0] maybe top. For each idx in indices:
      w=weights[idx]
      if w <= 1e-12 continue
      d = idx - (n-1)
      if d >= 0:
          L = n-d
          # term h[j](1 - h[j-d]) with j=d..n-1, i=j-d 0..L-1
          # grad h[0:L] -= h[d:L+d]? 
          grad[0:L] -= h[d:d+L]
          grad[d:d+L] += 1.0 - h[0:L]
      else:
          s=-d
          L=n-s
          # term h[i](1-h[i+s])
          grad[0:L] += 1.0 - h[s:s+L]
          grad[s:s+L] -= h[0:L]
   return grad * dx
 Check signs: derivative of q=dx*sum term. For positive d: corr=∑ h_{i+d}(1-h_i). dcorr/dh_i = -h_{i+d}; dh_{i+d}=1-h_i. Yes.
 For negative d: corr=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+s}); dh_i=1-h_{i+s}; dh_{i+s}=-h_i. Yes.
 This is gradient of C_w=∑ w_i q_i (w sum 1). Good.

Line search robust:

Pair move algorithm:
```
def pair_local_search(h, n, target, max_evals, rng, current_best_c):
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   best = compute_c5(h)
   ones? not needed
   evals=0
   improved=True
   while improved and evals<max_evals and time left:
      improved=False
      sweeps? random pairs
      for _ in range(num_pairs):
         i,j distinct
         tmin=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0); tmax=min(1-h[i], h[j])
         if tmax-tmin < 1e-8: continue
         # sample t: endpoints + mid + maybe current? We want move from current (t=0). Endpoints often good.
         for t in candidate_t:
            if abs(t)<1e-6: continue
            h_i_t=h[i]+t; h_j_t=h[j]-t
            if h_i_t<0 or >1... skip
            h[i]=h_i_t; h[j]=h_j_t
            c=compute_c5(h)
            if c < best - 1e-12:
                best=c; improved=True; break (keep)
            else:
                restore
         # maybe if no improve restore to best_h (it is)
   return h,best
```
Candidate t values: endpoints tmin,tmax, and maybe t to swap near values: t = -h[i] (set i to0), t=1-h[i] (set i to1), t=h[j] (set j to0), t=1-h[j] (set j to1) are endpoints. Also t = (target? no). Evaluate max count:
```
def bit_eval_max(bits,n):
    maxc=0
    for d in range(1,n):
        mask = (1 << (n-d)) -1  # precompute
        shifted = bits >> d
        c1 = (shifted & ~bits & mask).bit_count()
        c2 = (bits & ~shifted & mask).bit_count()
        if c1>maxc: maxc=c1
        if c2>maxc: maxc=c2
    return maxc
```
But ~bits infinite ones; need mask: & mask handles. For efficiency, precompute list masks. Also for d where n-d <= max_possible? We can't prune. This evaluation O(n) big int ops. For n=256, maybe 2-5 µs? Very fast. 1M evals ~5s? Good. We can do many local search moves.

We can also precompute for current bits? For each move, update max maybe could use histograms, but bit_eval enough.

`project_feasible(h, target)`:
```
def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        mask = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if np.any(mask):
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            h += diff / n
        np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    # final adjust one variable if residual
    s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h < 1.0)) if diff>0 else int(np.argmin(h > 0.0))
       # Need exact not violating.
    return h
```
Better final: If diff positive, find variable <1 and add min(diff,1-h[i]); if not enough loop. But residual tiny.

Actually if mask none and diff not zero due target impossible? target=n/2 within [0,2n] always possible. After clip, sum may be lower/upper; distribute. Use `h += diff/n` then clip may change; loop handles.

For exact final: after loop, diff may be e.g. 1e-15. We can adjust a non-bound variable by diff. If none, ignore.

`evaluate_c5(h)`:
```
def c5_of(h):
    n=h.size; dx=2.0/n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```

```
_MASK_CACHE = {}
def get_masks(n):
   if n not in _MASK_CACHE:
      masks=[]
      full=(1<<n)-1
      for d in range(1,n): masks.append((1 << (n-d))-1)
      _MASK_CACHE[n]=masks
   return _MASK_CACHE[n]
```

`bit_max_count(bits,n)`:
```
def bit_max_count(bits,n):
    masks = get_masks(n)
    maxc=0
    notfull = ~(bits) # infinite but we'll mask
    for d,mask in enumerate(masks, start=1):
        sh = bits >> d
        c1 = (sh & ~bits & mask).bit_count()
        c2 = (bits & ~sh & mask).bit_count()
        if c1>maxc: maxc=c1
        if c2>maxc: maxc=c2
    return maxc
```
Need `~bits` works with infinite ones, but after & mask finite. OK.

Could optimize by precomputing `inv_bits = ((1<<n)-1) ^ bits` then `sh & inv_bits & mask`, `bits & ~sh & mask` where `~sh` should be masked: use `inv_sh = full ^ (sh & full)`? For c2: bits & (~sh) & mask; since sh has bits beyond n maybe 0, ~sh infinite; mask handles. `bits & (full ^ sh) & mask` maybe faster. Let's use:
```
full = (1<<n)-1
for d,mask in enumerate(masks,1):
   sh = bits >> d
   c1 = (sh & (full ^ bits) & mask).bit_count()
   c2 = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
```
But `full ^ sh` only valid if sh within full (it is since bits n bits). This creates big int.

Maybe compute both using mismatch count: `(bits ^ sh) & mask` gives mismatches at distance d (undirected). But directional separate. We need directional max, not just sum. Could directional max ≤sum, but objective directional. If we only track sum, may accept bad directional. Need directional.

Acceptance:
```
def try_swap(bits, ones, zeros, h_arr, i,j, maxc, T, rng):
   if i==j: return None
   new_bits = bits ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
   newc = bit_max_count(new_bits, n)
   if newc < maxc or (T>0 and rng.random() < math.exp((maxc-newc)/T)):
       # accept
       bits = new_bits; update arrays/h_arr
   return bits, ones, zeros, h_arr, maxc
```
If newc == maxc? Could accept occasionally to move landscape. Maybe accept if newc <= maxc (no worse) with probability. For hill climbing, accept equal to avoid stuck. Use small probability for maxc+1.

SA schedule: start T = 2.0 (in units of count) down to 0 over iterations. Actually count differences integer. Temperature maybe 1.5 to 0.05. We can run loops until time.

Heuristic local search phases:
1. Greedy active-pair swap: For current maxc, compute all directional counts? `bit_counts(bits,n)` returns arrays pos, neg maybe. If we recompute counts each iteration. Then for top lags (pos/neg), try swaps on mismatched pairs. Accept if decreases. Repeat until no decrease. Counts recompute O(n) big ints; okay.
2. Random SA swaps.
3. Random restart: perturb bits with e.g. 5-20 random swaps starting from best, SA.

`bit_counts(bits,n)` similar to bit_max_count but store lists? Could be memory. For active, maybe compute top lags by storing max and also top arrays. n small, we can store pos/neg arrays length n-1 using list or np.array. Use Python list append counts.

```
def bit_counts(bits,n):
    masks=get_masks(n); full=(1<<n)-1; inv=full^bits
    pos=np.zeros(n-1,dtype=np.int32); neg=...
    for d,mask in enumerate(masks,1):
       sh=bits>>d
       pos[d-1] = (sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
       neg[d-1] = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
    return pos,neg
```
For local active, after each accept need recompute if trying active. Could compute once per inner loop.

Greedy active swap algorithm:
```
def binary_greedy_bits(bits,n,maxc,rng,max_iters,budget):
   ones=zeros etc
   for it in range(max_iters):
       if time: break
       pos,neg=bit_counts(bits,n)
       maxc = max(max(pos),max(neg))
       improved=False
       # top lags: indices sorted by max(pos+i,neg+i) descending maybe top 20
       vals = np.maximum(pos,neg)
       order = np.argsort(-vals, kind='stable')
       for idx in order:
           d=idx+1
           val=pos[idx]; # np pos[i+d](1-h_i)
           if val <= maxc: continue? high if val > maxc-1 maybe.
           # candidate lags with val close max: val >= maxc-1
           # find mismatched pairs:
           if val > neg[idx] and val >= maxc-5: try pos pairs (i zero, i+d one)
           if neg[idx] > ...: try neg pairs (i one, i+d zero)
       if not improved: break/SA
```
When trying active pair i,j: if bits pattern exactly term=1, swap will reduce that term by 1 but may increase others. We can just swap and evaluate.

```
def run_smooth_gradient(h0,n,tau,max_secs,verbose):
  h=project(h0,n/2)
  dx=2/n
  h_prev = None; no_improve=0
  step_scale = 0.1
  iter=0
  while time left:

    q = corr*dx
    qmax = q.max()
    # if qmax <= best_c? still
    # weights
    x = (q - qmax)/tau
    x = np.clip(x, -50, 0)
    w = np.exp(x); w /= w.sum()
    # ensure if too sparse add top uniform? maybe w = 0.9*softmax +0.1*topM?

    # choose step. We'll evaluate several steps

    best_trial = None; best_S = S_cur; best_step=0

       trial = project(h - step*grad, n/2)
       corr_t = np.correlate(trial,1-trial,'full'); q_t=corr_t*dx
       # smooth value

    if best_trial: h=best_trial; ... iter count; no_improve=0

  return h,best
```

Weighted gradient function:
```
def weighted_gradient(weights, h, n):
    g = np.zeros(n)
    center = n-1
    # for idx, w in enumerate(weights):
    nz = np.nonzero(weights > 1e-10)[0]
    for idx in nz:
        w = weights[idx]
        d = idx - center
        if d >= 0:
            L = n - d
            if L <=0: continue
            g[:L] -= h[d:d+L]
            g[d:d+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
        else:
            s = -d
            L = n - s
            g[:L] += 1.0 - h[s:s+L]
            g[s:s+L] -= h[:L]
    return g * (2.0/n)
```
This computes gradient of weighted q where weights sum maybe 1. Good.

We should be mindful: `g[:L] -= h[d:d+L]` for d=0? But lag d=0 q=∑h(1-h) gradient =1-2h. In our formula for d>=0 with d=0: L=n; g[:n] -= h; g[0:n] +=1-h => 1-2h. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def improve_pair(h,i,j,cur_c,target, rng):
  tmin = max(-h[i], h[j]-1)
  tmax = min(1-h[i], h[j])
  if tmax-tmin < 1e-8: return False
  fractions = [0.0,1.0,0.5, 1/3,2/3, (h[j]-h[i])/(2*(tmax-tmin))? not if range]
  candidates=[tmin + f*(tmax-tmin) for f in fractions]
  candidates += [0.5-h[i], -(0.5-h[j])] # t to i=0.5? t=0.5-h_i; j=0.5 -> t=h_j-0.5
  unique within bounds
  best_c=cur_c; best_t=0
  orig_i, orig_j = h[i], h[j]
  for t in candidates:
     if abs(t)<1e-7: continue
     hi=orig_i+t; hj=orig_j-t
     if hi<...: continue
     h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
     c=c5_of(h)
     if c < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_c=c; best_t=t
     h[i]=orig_i; h[j]=orig_j
  if best_t !=0:
     h[i]=orig_i+best_t; h[j]=orig_j-best_t
     return best_c
  return False
```
Candidate t that sets both 0.5 if sum=1: t=(1 - orig_i? wait h_i'+h_j'=same sum S=hi+hj. t makes hi=0.5 => t=0.5-hi; hj=S-0.5=1-hi? okay).

Let's implement binary local search more carefully for speed:
- Precompute masks list. In `bit_max_count`, local variables for speed.
```
def bit_max_count(bits, n):
    masks = MASK_CACHE.get(n)
    maxc=0
    full = (1<<n)-1
    inv = full ^ bits
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, 1):
        sh = bits >> d
        c1 = (sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
        if c1 > maxc: maxc = c1
        c2 = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
        if c2 > maxc: maxc = c2
    return maxc
```
`full ^ sh` each loop. Could use `(~sh) & mask` simpler? `(~sh) & mask` uses negative int but bitwise. Maybe faster: `(bits & ~sh & mask)`. Since mask bounds, Python handles. Use `~sh`. Similarly `sh & ~bits & mask`. Avoid full xor allocation. Test unknown. Use `~` might create negative infinite, but & mask yields. It might be okay. For speed, `full ^ sh` positive maybe faster. We'll use positive full.

Could compute mismatches and orient? Need both. Fine.

`binary_greedy_and_sa` algorithm:
```
def binary_local_search(bits,n,budget_sec,rng):
    m=n//2
    bits=fix_bit_count(bits,n,m)
    best_bits=bits; best_c=bit_max_count(bits,n)
    # build helpers
    ones = [i for i in range(n) if (bits>>i)&1]
    zeros = [i for i in range(n) if not ...]
    pos1 = [-1]*n; pos0=[-1]*n; fill
    h_arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8); fill
    current? We can work in-place; keep best.
    T=2.0
    stall=0
    kicks=0
    while remaining_time()>5:
       # if current too far from best or stall, reset/perturb
       # compute counts once per outer? We'll do active greedy for some iterations
       pos_arr, neg_arr = bit_counts(bits,n)
       vals = np.maximum(pos_arr, neg_arr)
       current_c = int(np.max(vals))
       if current_c < best_c: update best
       # target top lags: idxs where vals >= vals.max()-1? top 20
       order = np.argsort(-vals)
       improved=False
       for idx in order:
          d=idx+1
          # if pos high and > neg: pattern pos (zero at i, one at i+d)
          if pos_arr[idx] > maxc - 10 and pos_arr[idx] >=5: # active
             pattern = (bits >> d) & (( (1<<n)-1) ^ bits) & ((1 << (n-d))-1)
             while pattern:
                lsb = pattern & -pattern
                i = lsb.bit_length()-1
                pattern ^= lsb
                j = i+d
                # swap i(0) j(1): Actually i is base zero, j one. Swap them makes i=1, j=0; reduces pos term.
                newc = try swap...
                if newc < current_c: accept; improved=True; current_c=newc; break inner?
          if neg similar pattern = bits & ~(bits>>d) & mask (i one, j? if neg term h_i(1-h_{i+d}), i one, i+d zero; swap i,j)
       if not improved:
          # random SA stage: do e.g. 200 swaps
          for _ in range(200):
             random one_idx, zero_idx; i=ones[...], j=zeros[...]
             do swap eval newc
             delta=newc-current_c
             if delta<0 or (T>0 and rng.random()<math.exp(-delta/T)):
                 accept; current_c=newc
             T=max(0.05, T*0.995)
          if current_c >= best_c and no improvement many: reheat T=1.5, reset to best or kick
    return best_bits,best_c
```

```
def apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j):
    # i one, j zero or i zero j one; just flip both
    bits ^= (1<<i) | (1<<j)
    if h_arr[i] == 1:
       pi=pos1[i]; pj=pos0[j]
       ones[pi]=j; pos1[j]=pi; pos0[j]=-1
       zeros[pj]=i; pos0[i]=pj; pos1[i]=-1
       h_arr[i]=0; h_arr[j]=1
    else:
       # i zero, j one
    return bits
```

Global:
```
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_TAU = 1.0
def slsqp_obj(x):
   n=_OPT_N; dx=2.0/n; q=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*dx; qmax=float(q.max()); z=(q-qmax)/_OPT_TAU; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); s=float(e.sum()); val=_OPT_TAU*(qmax + math.log(s)); return val
def slsqp_grad(x):
   n=_OPT_N; dx=2.0/n; q=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*dx; qmax=float(q.max()); z=(q-qmax)/_OPT_TAU; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); w=e/e.sum(); return weighted_gradient(w,x,n)
def sum_con_fun(x): return np.sum(x) - _OPT_N/2.0
```
Weights: `e/e.sum()` sum 1. If sum zero? e max 1. Good.
`slsqp_polish(h0,n,tau_list,time_limit)`:

```
   if remaining_time()<10: break
   set _OPT_N, _OPT_TAU
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con_fun}]
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   try: res=minimize(slsqp_obj, h0, jac=slsqp_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':200,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
   except: pass
   h=project_feasible(res.x,n/2); c=c5_of(h); if c<best update
```

```
    idx = np.argsort(q)[ -topm:]
```

`slsqp_polish` code:
```
def slsqp_polish(h0,n,tau_list,time_budget):
  h=project_feasible(h0,n/2); best=h; bestc=c5_of(h)
  global _OPT_N,_OPT_TAU
  for tau in tau_list:
    if time.time() - start > time_budget: break
    _OPT_N=n; _OPT_TAU=tau
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun': sum_constraint}]
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    maxiter = 300 if n<=200 else 100
    try:
       res = minimize(slsqp_function, h, jac=slsqp_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
    except Exception:
       continue
    if res.x is not None:
      h2=project_feasible(res.x,n/2); c2=c5_of(h2)
      if c2<bestc: best=...; h=h2; bestc=c2
  return best,bestc
```

```
def pair_local_search(h0,n,best_c,rng,deadline):
   h=project_feasible(h0,n/2); cur=c5_of(h); best=cur
   active_top = None; active_counter=0
   use_active = n <= 320
   no_block = 0
   block_improve = False
   while time.time() < deadline - 2:
      # block of 60 pair attempts
      block_improve = False
      if use_active and active_counter == 0:
          corr = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); q=corr*(2/n)
          # top indices with weights: argsort maybe
          order = np.argsort(q)[-30:]
          # build probability? just random choose from order every time
      for _ in range(60):
         if use_active and len(active_top):
             idx = active_top[rng.randint(len(active_top))]
             d = idx - (n-1)
             if d >=0:
                 i = rng.randint(n-d)
                 j = i + d
             else:
                 s=-d; i = rng.randint(n-s); j = i + s
         else:
             i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
         if i==j: continue
         # improve pair
         old_i=h[i]; old_j=h[j]
         # generate candidate t set
         cand = ...
         for t in cand:
             if abs(t) < 1e-7: continue
             hi=old_i+t; hj=old_j-t
             if hi< -1e-9 or hi>1+1e-9 or hj<...: continue
             h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
             c=c5_of(h)
             if c < cur - 1e-12:
                cur=c; block_improve=True; break
             # restore
         # if no improvement, h restored if loop didn't keep? Need restore after each; if found, keep and break.
         # if no found, restore old values.
         if not block_improve_for_pair: # but block_improve global lost; use local improved_pair
             h[i]=old_i; h[j]=old_j
         if block_improve:
             if cur < best: best=cur; best_h=h.copy()
       active_counter +=1
       if block_improve:
          no_block=0; active_counter=0
          # recompute active after some improvements? set active_top=None for next block
          active_top = None
       else:
          no_block +=1
          if no_block > 30: # 1800 attempts no improve
             # perturb h
             perturbed = h + 0.1*rng.randn(n)* (rng.rand(n)<0.1?)
             perturbed=project_feasible(perturbed,n/2)
             c_p=c5_of(perturbed)
             # Only replace current if not too bad: c_p <= best + 0.02 or random
             if c_p < best + 0.01 or rng.rand()<0.2:
                h=perturbed; cur=c_p
             best? keep best
             active_top=None; no_block=0
   return best_h,best
```

```
def remaining_time(safety=2.0):
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET_S - (time.time() - _START_TIME) - safety)
```

`project_feasible` final exactness:
```
def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if np.any(mask):
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            h += diff / n
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
                if add > 0:
                    h[idx] += add; diff -= add
                    if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
            else:
                sub = min(h[idx], -diff)
                if sub > 0:
                    h[idx] -= sub; diff += sub
                    if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
    return h
```
If diff positive but all at 1? target impossible but not.

`max_corr_of`:
```
def max_corr_of(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')))
```
If h empty? no.

`c5_of`: `return max_corr_of(h)*2.0/h.size`

`try_cyclic_shifts`:
```
def try_cyclic_shifts(h, n, max_evals=None):
    if n <= 0: return h, max_corr_of(h)
    h = np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_mc = max_corr_of(h)
    if n > 512 or max_evals is not None and max_evals < n:
       nums = np.unique(np.linspace(0,n-1,num=max_evals).astype(int))
    else:
       nums = range(n)
    for s in nums:
       if s ==0: continue
       sh = np.roll(h, int(s), axis=0)
       mc = max_corr_of(sh)
       if mc < best_mc - 1e-15:
           best_mc=mc; best_h=sh.copy()
    return best_h,best_mc
```
But doing all shifts may be expensive for n=400. We'll call with limit maybe 80. For parent n=160 all. In run, limit = min(n, 120) maybe.

`make_sine`: if n even? x centers. `x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n`; `h=0.5+amp*np.sin(math.pi*freq*x+phase)`; project. Need amplitude maybe <0.5 to stay [0,1]; 0.5 exact. Project may alter.

`make_random_h`: `h = 0.5 + 0.4*rng.normal(size=n)`, clip, project.

`bits_to_array`:
```
def bits_to_array(bits,n):
    arr=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
       if (bits>>i)&1: arr[i]=1.0
    return arr
```

`array_to_bits`:
```
def array_to_bits(arr):
    bits=0
    for i,v in enumerate(arr):
       if v: bits |= (1<<i)
    return bits
```
If arr bool/int. For continuous, threshold function.

`bits_from_scores`:
```
def bits_from_scores(n, scores, rng):
    m=n//2
    scores = np.asarray(scores,float)
    # add tiny random to break ties
    scores = scores + 1e-9*rng.rand(n)
    idx = np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:m]
    arr=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); arr[idx]=1
    return array_to_bits(arr)
```

Score functions:
- random: rng.rand(n)
- Thue-Morse: parity of i. Could use np? Python loop or vector with bit_count? For n small, loop. `scores[i] = 1 if bin(i).count('1')%2 else 0`. But want 0/1; density half. Good.
- Golden: maybe `scores = -np.abs(((np.arange(n)+0.5)/n * ((1+sqrt5)/2) %1) - 0.5)`? Higher near 0.5? Does that produce low overlap? Unknown. Use as seed only.
- Parent-threshold: scores = parent interpolated.

`fix_bit_count`:
```
def fix_bit_count(bits,n,m,rng):
    cnt=bits.bit_count()
    while cnt != m and attempts:
       if cnt > m:
          # remove random one
          r=rng.randint(n)
          if (bits>>r)&1:
             bits ^= 1<<r; cnt-=1
       else:
          r=rng.randint(n)
          if not ((bits>>r)&1):
             bits |= 1<<r; cnt+=1
    return bits
```
For n=256, random fine. Could be infinite if weird; limit 10000.

`get_masks`:
```
def get_masks(n):
    cached = _MASK_CACHE.get(n)
    if cached is None:
       cached=[(1 << (n-d)) - 1 for d in range(1,n)]
       _MASK_CACHE[n]=cached
    return cached
```
`1 << (n-d)` okay for n up to 1024.

`bit_max_count` optimized:
```
def bit_max_count(bits,n):
    masks = get_masks(n)
    maxc=0
    inv = ((1<<n)-1) ^ bits
    # but full^sh in loop. Could compute full each.
    full = (1<<n)-1
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, 1):
        sh = bits >> d
        c1 = (sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
        if c1 > maxc: maxc = c1
        c2 = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
        if c2 > maxc: maxc = c2
    return maxc
```
`bit_counts`:
```
def bit_counts(bits,n):
    masks=get_masks(n); full=(1<<n)-1; inv=full^bits
    pos=np.zeros(n-1,dtype=np.int32); neg=np.zeros(n-1,dtype=np.int32)
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks,1):
       sh=bits>>d
       pos[d-1]=(sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
       neg[d-1]=(bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
    return pos,neg
```

`apply_swap` as above. Need update `h_arr` maybe uint8. For active patterns, h_arr not needed except apply. We'll maintain but can update after swap from bits? We know i status. Use h_arr.

```
def apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j):
    if i == j: return bits
    mask = (1<<i) | (1<<j)
    newbits = bits ^ mask
    if h_arr[i] == 1:
        pi = pos1[i]; pj = pos0[j]
        ones[pi] = j; pos1[j] = pi; pos0[j] = -1
        zeros[pj] = i; pos0[i] = pj; pos1[i] = -1
        h_arr[i] = 0; h_arr[j] = 1
    else:
        pi = pos0[i]; pj = pos1[j]
        zeros[pi] = j; pos0[j] = pi; pos1[j] = -1
        ones[pj] = i; pos1[i] = pj; pos0[i] = -1
        h_arr[i] = 1; h_arr[j] = 0
    return newbits
```
Assumes one/zero correct.

`continuous_polish`:
```
def continuous_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
  h=project_feasible(h0,n/2.0)
  # cyclic shift best
  h,mc = try_cyclic_shifts(h,n, max_evals=min(n,80)); best_h=h; best_mc=mc
  # SLSQP if enough time
  if remaining_for(deadline)>30:
     taus = [0.02,0.01,0.004,0.001] maybe depending n
     h2,mc2 = slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,rng)
     update
  # pair local
  if time < deadline:
     h2,mc2 = pair_local_search(h,n,deadline,rng)
     update
  # after pair, maybe SLSQP again quickly if time
  if time < deadline-20:
     h2,mc2 = slsqp_polish(h,n, deadline-5, rng, tau_list=[0.002,0.0008])
  return best_h,best_mc
```

Now implement `slsqp_polish` with deadlines and time. Need not use rng.

```
def slsqp_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n/2.0)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_mc = max_corr_of(h)
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_TAU
    taus = [0.03,0.01,0.004,0.0015]
    if n > 240: taus = [0.03,0.01,0.003]
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_con}]
    for tau in taus:
       if time.time() >= deadline - 5 or remaining_time()<5: break
       _OPT_N=n; _OPT_TAU=tau
       maxiter = 120 if n>200 else 250
       # time per tau maybe limit by local time. We cannot limit SciPy call except maxiter. OK.
       try:
          res = minimize(slsqp_obj, h, jac=slsqp_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
       except Exception:
          continue
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
          h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n/2.0)
          mc2 = max_corr_of(h2)
          if mc2 < best_mc - 1e-12:
             best_mc=mc2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2
          else:
             # maybe still h improved exact? no
             pass
    return best_h,best_mc
```
Potential SLSQP may take long for n=256. maxiter 120. OK.

```
   q=...
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)): return 1e6
```

Now `pair_local_search` details. Let's write robust code.

```
def pair_candidate_ts(hi, hj):
    tmin = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    tmax = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
    if tmax - tmin < 1e-8: return []
    cands = [tmin, tmax, 0.5*(tmin+tmax)]
    span = tmax - tmin
    for f in (0.25,0.33,0.66,0.75):
       cands.append(tmin + f*span)
    t = 0.5 - hi; if tmin < t < tmax: cands.append(t)
    t = hj - 0.5; if tmin < t < tmax: cands.append(t)
    t = 0.5*(hj - hi) # hi+h? hi+t = hj-t => t=(hj-hi)/2
    if tmin < t < tmax: cands.append(t)
    # unique with tol 1e-6
    uniq=[]
    for t in cands:
       if tmin -1e-9 <= t <= tmax+1e-9:
          tc = min(max(t,tmin),tmax)
          duplicate=False
          for u in uniq:
             if abs(u-tc)<1e-6: duplicate=True; break
          if not duplicate: uniq.append(tc)
    return uniq
```
`hj - hi`? Wait if h_i'=h_i+t, h_j'=h_j-t. Set equal: hi+t = hj-t => t=(hj-hi)/2. Good.

`pair_local_search`:
```
def pair_local_search(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n/2.0)
    cur_mc = max_corr_of(h)
    best_mc = cur_mc
    best_h = h.copy()
    top_list = None
    use_active = True
    block_no = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 3 and remaining_time()>3:
       if top_list is None:
           corr = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
           order = np.argsort(corr)[-40:]
           # If all equal? np.argsort fill.
           top_list = [int(x) for x in order]
       block_improved = False
       for _ in range(70):
           if time.time() >= deadline-3: break
           if top_list:
              idx = top_list[rng.randint(len(top_list))]
              d = idx - (n-1)
              if d >= 0:
                 if n-d <=0: continue
                 i = int(rng.randint(n-d)); j=i+d
              else:
                 s=-d
                 if n-s <=0: continue
                 i = int(rng.randint(n-s)); j=i+s
           else:
              i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
           if i==j: continue
           hi0 = h[i]; hj0 = h[j]
           cands = pair_candidate_ts(hi0,hj0)
           improved_pair = False
           for t in cands:
              if abs(t) < 1e-7: continue
              hi = hi0 + t; hj = hj0 - t
              if hi < -1e-9 or hi > 1+1e-9 or hj < -1e-9 or hj > 1+1e-9: continue
              h[i]=hi; h[j]=hj
              mc = max_corr_of(h)
              if mc < cur_mc - 1e-12:
                  cur_mc = mc
                  improved_pair = True
                  break
              # else restore
           if improved_pair:
              if cur_mc < best_mc:
                  best_mc=cur_mc; best_h=h.copy()
              block_improved=True
              # recompute top next block
              top_list=None
              break  # maybe break block after one improvement to recompute active? Could continue current h.
           else:
              # restore original if no improvement
              h[i]=hi0; h[j]=hj0
       if not block_improved:
          block_no +=1
          if block_no >= 3:
             # perturbation
             h = project_feasible(h + 0.12*rng.normal(size=n)*(rng.random_sample(n) < 0.15), n/2.0)
             cur_mc = max_corr_of(h)
             # if too bad, revert to best
             if cur_mc > best_mc + 0.05:
                h = best_h.copy(); cur_mc=best_mc
             top_list=None
             block_no=0
       else:
          block_no=0
    return best_h, best_mc
```
This may evaluate max_corr for each candidate. If `improved_pair`, we break out of block; good. If pair had a candidate improving after some candidates, we leave h at improved and don't restore. If no, restore. Candidate list order includes tmin/tmax first maybe setting endpoints. If endpoint improves, we stop. Good.

But if first candidate worsens, restore for each; after loop restore original if no improvement (already original maybe). Fine.

Potential sum error: Setting h[i]=hi, h[j]=hj preserves sum within rounding. After many accepted, sum may drift due rounding (tiny). We can project occasionally? If we project, sum exact but values change. We can after each improvement not project; pair c5_of doesn't enforce sum; evaluator will check sum exactly? Need final sum exactly. Drift 1e-15 okay. But constraints require sum(h)*dx=1 equivalently sum==n/2 exactly? They likely use tolerance. We can project at end. During pair, sum drift negligible. To be safe, every 100 improvements project? Can degrade. At end.

`continuous_polish` uses pair to a deadline. It then maybe SLSQP again. Let's write:
```
def continuous_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n/2.0)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_mc=max_corr_of(h)
    # try shifts/sym quick
    sh,mc = try_cyclic_shifts(h,n, min(n,80)); if mc<best_mc: update
    sy=symmetrize(h); if sum? project; mc=... update
    # SLSQP
    if local_time < 20:
       h2,mc2 = slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline-5,rng)
       update
    # pair
    if local_time > 5:
       h2,mc2 = pair_local_search(h,n,deadline,rng)
       update
    # final SLSQP short if time
    if local_time > 20:
       h2,mc2 = slsqp_polish(h,n, min(deadline, time.time()+10), rng)
       update
    return project_feasible(best_h,n/2.0), best_mc
```
`try_cyclic_shifts` returns copy and mc. Need if n large, limit 40.

```
def choose_best_initial_bits(n,hstart,rng):
   scores_h = hstart
   bits = []
   bits.append(bits_from_scores(n,scores_h,rng))
   bits.append(bits_from_scores(n,thue_morse_scores(n),rng))
   bits.append(bits_from_scores(n,golden_scores(n),rng))

       b=fix_bit_count(b,n,n//2,rng)
       c=bit_max_count(b,n)

   # a few random swaps to each? no.
```

```
def parity(i):
   p=0
   while i: p^=i&1; i>>=1
   return p
```

```
def projected_gradient_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
   h=project_feasible; best
   for tau in [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
      if time > deadline: break
      h_local=h
      noimp=0
      step_base = 0.05
      for it in range(300):
         if time > deadline-5 or remaining<3: break
         corr=np.correlate(h_local,1-h_local,'full'); q=corr*(2/n)
         qmax=q.max(); z=(q-qmax)/tau; clip; e=exp; w=e/e.sum()
         grad=weighted_gradient(w,h_local,n)
         gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
         if gnorm<1e-10: break
         # Try steps absolute [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5] maybe scaled
         cur_S = tau*(qmax+math.log(e.sum()))
         best_trial=None; best_S=cur_S; best_mc=qmax exact
         for s in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]:
             step=s* (0.5 + 0.5? no)
             trial=project_feasible(h_local - step*grad, n/2)
             corr_t=...; q_t=...; qtmax=...
             z_t=(q_t-qtmax)/tau; e_t=...; St=...
             mc_t=qtmax
             if mc_t < best_mc and St < cur_S:
                 best_trial=trial; best_S=St; best_mc=mc_t; break # choose first improving exact? maybe
             elif St < best_S - 1e-12 and mc_t <= qmax + 1e-6:
                 best_trial=trial; best_S=St; best_mc=mc_t
         if best_trial is not None:
             h_local=best_trial; noimp=0; update best global
         else:
             noimp +=1
             if noimp > 20: break
      # after tau, maybe random small perturb? no
   return best
```

```
def smooth_value(q,tau):
   qmax=float(np.max(q)); z=(q-qmax)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); return tau*(qmax + math.log(e.sum()))
```

`projected_gradient_polish` detailed:
```
def projected_gradient_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
   target=n/2.0
   h=project_feasible(h0,target); best_h=h.copy(); best_mc=max_corr_of(h); cur_mc=best_mc
   dx=2.0/n
   for tau in [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.004,0.0015,0.0006]:
      if time.time() >= deadline - 5 or remaining_time()<5: break
      h_cur=h
      noimp=0
      for it in range(400):
         if time.time() >= deadline - 5 or remaining_time()<3: break
         corr=np.correlate(h_cur,1-h_cur,'full'); q=corr*dx
         qmax=float(np.max(q))
         # if qmax < best_mc? update? best_mc exact.
         z=(q-qmax)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: continue; w=e/s
         grad=weighted_gradient(w,h_cur,n)
         gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
         if gnorm < 1e-11: break
         # step proposals absolute; scale inversely with gnorm? Use raw steps times (1.0/(1+gnorm/10))?
         scale = 1.0 / (1.0 + gnorm/5.0)
         tried=False
         for base in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
             step=base*scale
             if step<1e-5: continue
             trial=project_feasible(h_cur - step*grad, target)
             corr_t=np.correlate(trial,1-trial,'full'); qt=corr_t*dx; qt_max=float(np.max(qt))
             # smooth trial
             st=smooth_value(qt,tau)
             sc=smooth_value(q,tau)
             if qt_max < qmax - 1e-12 and st < sc - 1e-12:
                 h_cur=trial; cur_mc=qt_max; tried=True; break
             if st < sc - 1e-10 and qt_max <= qmax + 0.002:
                 # tentatively? choose best
                 if trial_c < cur_mc? ...
         if tried: noimp=0
         else:
            noimp +=1
            if noimp > 30: break
      # after tau, set h maybe to best if best better? We can keep best_h and also h_cur?
      if cur_mc < best_mc:
          best_mc=cur_mc; best_h=h_cur.copy()
      else:
          # If best_h better, use best_h for next tau?
          h = best_h.copy()
          cur_mc = best_mc
   return best_h,best_mc
```

```
def continuous_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):

   if time.time() < t_end-20:
      h2,mc2 = slsqp_polish(h,n,t_end-3,rng) if n<=200 else pair...
   return best
```

Let's implement smooth functions:
```
def softmax_weights(q, tau):
    qmax=float(np.max(q))
    z=(q-qmax)/tau
    np.clip(z,-50,0,out=z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300: w=np.full_like(q,1.0/(2*n-1)) ...
    return qmax, e/s
```
But `np.clip` with out if z dtype. Good.

`projected_gradient_polish` will call softmax each iter. To reduce double corr for line search, we compute q current once. For trial, need q. Good.

```
def local_time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
```

`make_sine`:
```
def make_sine(n, freq, phase, amp=0.5):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    h=0.5 + amp*np.sin(math.pi*freq*x + phase)
    # if freq integer and phase 0, average maybe 0.5. Project.
    return project_feasible(h, n/2.0)
```
Need if amp=0.5 exactly values maybe 0/1. Good.

`make_random_h`:
```
def make_random_h(n,rng,centered=True):
    h = 0.5 + 0.45*rng.normal(size=n)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return project_feasible(h, n/2.0)
```

`thue_morse_scores`:
```
def thue_morse_scores(n):
   s=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n):
      v=i; p=0
      while v:
         p ^= (v & 1); v >>=1
      s[i]= float(p)
   return s
```
Could vectorize with bit_count? Keep loop.

`golden_scores`:
```
def golden_scores(n):
   gr=(1+math.sqrt(5))/2
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   frac=(x*gr) % 1.0
   return -np.abs(frac - 0.5)
```
Maybe add random in bits_from_scores.

`van der corput`? Not needed.

`choose_best_initial_bits` uses `bits_from_scores`. Need `fix_bit_count`.

`slsqp_con` uses `_OPT_N`:
```
def slsqp_con(x):
   return np.sum(x) - 0.5*float(_OPT_N)
```

`slsqp_obj` and `slsqp_grad`:
```
def slsqp_obj(x):
   n=_OPT_N
   if n<=0: return 0.0
   dx=2.0/n
   corr=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')
   q=corr*dx
   qmax=float(np.max(q))
   z=(q-qmax)/_OPT_TAU
   z=z if np.all(np.isfinite(z)) else np.zeros_like(z)
   np.clip(z,-50,0,out=z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(e.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300: return qmax
   return _OPT_TAU*(qmax + math.log(s))
```
If qmax NaN? Check `if not np.isfinite(qmax): return 1e6`.

`slsqp_grad` similar; if not finite return zeros.

`weighted_gradient` use `if d >= 0:` d can be 0. For idx loop, `weights` float maybe. Use `for idx in nz:`.

`projected_gradient_polish` can use `softmax_weights` function.

```
def softmax_weights(q,tau):
    qmax=float(np.max(q))
    z=(q-qmax)/tau
    np.clip(z,-50,0,out=z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       w=np.zeros_like(q); w[np.argmax(q)]=1.0; return qmax,w
    w=e/s
    return qmax,w
```

`smooth_q_value(q,tau)` returns qmax+tau log sum; can use `softmax_weights`? Avoid double. Implement separately.

`continuous_polish`:
```
def continuous_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    target=0.5*n
    h=project_feasible(h0,target)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_of(h)
    t_end = min(deadline, _START_TIME+_BUDGET_S-4)
    # quick shifts
    if local_time_left(t_end)>5:
       sh,mc=try_cyclic_shifts(h,n, min(n,60)); c=mc*2/n
       if c<best_c: update h=sh
       sy=project_feasible(symmetrize(h),target); c=c5_of(sy); update
    total = max(1.0, t_end-time.time())
    pg_end = t_end
    if total > 80:
       pg_end = time.time() + 0.45*total
       pg_end = min(pg_end, t_end-30)
    if time.time() < pg_end and remaining_time()>10:
       h2,c2=projected_gradient_polish(h,n,pg_end,rng); if c2<best_c update
    # SLSQP
    if n <= 240 and time.time() < t_end-30 and remaining_time()>25:
       sl_deadline = time.time() + 0.2*max(30, t_end-time.time())
       sl_deadline = min(sl_deadline, t_end-10)
       h2,c2=slsqp_polish(h,n,sl_deadline,rng); update
    # pair
    if time.time() < t_end-5 and remaining_time()>8:
       pair_deadline = min(t_end-2, time.time()+max(10,0.5*(t_end-time.time())))
       h2,c2=pair_local_search(h,n,pair_deadline,rng); update
    # final short solver if time
    if remaining_time()>25 and time.time() < t_end-25:
       if n <= 200:
          h2,c2=slsqp_polish(h,n,t_end-3,rng); update
       else:
          h2,c2=pair_local_search(h,n,t_end-3,rng); update
    return project_feasible(best_h,target), best_c
```
This may allocate pair half of remaining after SLSQP; maybe okay. But if SLSQP consumes, pair less. Need ensure all phases use time. If total small, skip PG. Good.

However `continuous_polish` for each candidate n repeatedly may call SLSQP many times. In run, total time limited. OK.

```
def binary_local_search(bits,n,deadline,rng):
    m=n//2
    bits=fix_bit_count(bits,n,m,rng)
    ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,h_arr = build_bit_structs(bits,n)
    best_bits=bits; best_c=bit_max_count(bits,n); current_c=best_c
    full=(1<<n)-1; masks=get_masks(n)
    T=2.0; reset_gap=0; best_update_swaps=0; last_best_gap=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 2 and remaining_time()>2:
        if current_c - best_c > 8 or (time.time()-? no) or (best_update_gap > 2500):
            bits = best_bits
            ones,zeros,pos1,pos0,h_arr = build_bit_structs(bits,n)
            current_c = best_c
            T = 2.0
            best_update_gap=0
        # active greedy
        pos_arr,neg_arr = bit_counts(bits,n)
        vals = np.maximum(pos_arr, neg_arr)
        current_c = int(np.max(vals)) if n>1 else 0
        if current_c < best_c:
            best_c=current_c; best_bits=bits.copy? bits int immutable; best_bits=bits; best_update_gap=0
        improved=False
        order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]

        for ii in order[:40]:
            d=ii+1
            if vals[ii] <= max(2,current_c-3): break? If vals low, rest lower; but current_c maybe. Use if vals[ii] < current_c - 2: break.
            # pos
            if pos_arr[ii] > 2:
                mask = masks[ii]
                sh = bits >> d
                pattern = sh & inv & mask
                tries=0
                while pattern and tries < 40:
                    lsb = pattern & -pattern
                    i = lsb.bit_length()-1
                    pattern ^= lsb
                    tries += 1
                    j = i + d
                    newbits = bits ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
                    newc = bit_max_count(newbits,n)
                    if newc < current_c:
                        bits = apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1,pos0,h_arr,i,j)
                        current_c = newc
                        if newc < best_c: update
                        improved=True
                        break
                    elif newc == current_c and rng.random()<0.02:
                        bits = apply_swap(...); current_c=newc; improved=True; break
                if improved: break
            if neg_arr[ii] > 2 and not improved:
                mask=masks[ii]
                sh=bits >> d
                pattern = bits & (full ^ sh) & mask
                tries=0
                while pattern and tries<40:
                   ...
        if improved:
            continue
        # random SA
        sa_count=300
        for _ in range(sa_count):
            if time.time() >= deadline-2: break
            if not ones or not zeros: break
            oi=int(rng.randint(len(ones))); zi=int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))
            i=ones[oi]; j=zeros[zi]
            newbits = bits ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
            newc=bit_max_count(newbits,n)
            delta = newc-current_c
            accept = delta < 0
            if not accept and T > 0.01 and delta <= 6:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-delta/T): accept=True
            if accept:
                bits=apply_swap(... i,j); current_c=newc
                if newc < best_c: update
                best_update_gap += 1
                if newc <= best_c + 1: pass
            T = max(0.05, T*0.995)
        # decay T further and record gap if no best improvement? Need best_update_gap counts swaps since best. It gets reset on best. For delta<0 but not best? If best is min, delta<0 implies new current lower but may still > best. We should count all swaps? Use `swaps_since_best` increment every accept, reset if newc < best_c. If > threshold reset.
```

```
def softmax_smooth(q,tau):
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    z = (q - qmax) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)

       w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
       return qmax,w,qmax
    w = e/s
    return qmax,w,qmax + tau*math.log(s)
```

```
for x in lst:
   if x not in seen:
       seen.add(x); arr.append(x)
```
No lambda.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_START_TIME = 0.0
_BUDGET_S = 1300.0
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_TAU = 1.0
_MASK_CACHE = {}


def remaining_time(safety=2.0):
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET_S - (time.time() - _START_TIME) - safety)


def local_time_left(deadline):
    return max(0.0, deadline - time.time())


def project_feasible(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if np.any(mask):
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            h += diff / float(n)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if diff > 0:
                add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
                if add > 0:
                    h[idx] += add
                    diff -= add
                    if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                        break
            else:
                sub = min(h[idx], -diff)
                if sub > 0:
                    h[idx] -= sub
                    diff += sub
                    if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                        break
    return h


def max_corr_of(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))


def c5_of(h):
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return max_corr_of(h) * (2.0 / n)


def interp_to(src_h, src_n, dst_n):
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return np.asarray(src_h, dtype=float).copy()
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / src_n)
    xq = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / dst_n)
    return np.interp(xq, xp, np.asarray(src_h, dtype=float))


def symmetrize(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])


def make_sine(n, freq, phase, amp=0.5):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(math.pi * freq * x + phase)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)


def make_random_h(n, rng):
    h = 0.5 + 0.45 * rng.normal(size=n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)


def try_cyclic_shifts(h, n, max_shifts=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_mc = max_corr_of(h)
    if n <= 1:
        return best_h, best_mc
    if max_shifts is None:
        max_shifts = n
    max_shifts = int(min(n, max_shifts))
    if max_shifts <= 0:
        return best_h, best_mc
    if max_shifts >= n:
        shifts = range(n)
    else:
        shifts = np.unique(np.linspace(0, n - 1, max_shifts, dtype=int))
    for s in shifts:
        s = int(s)
        if s == 0:
            continue
        sh = np.roll(h, s, axis=0)
        mc = max_corr_of(sh)
        if mc < best_mc - 1e-15:
            best_mc = mc
            best_h = sh.copy()
    return best_h, best_mc


def bits_to_array(bits, n):
    arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if (bits >> i) & 1:
            arr[i] = 1.0
    return arr


def array_to_bits(arr):
    bits = 0
    for i, v in enumerate(arr):
        if v:
            bits |= (1 << i)
    return bits


def get_masks(n):
    cached = _MASK_CACHE.get(n)
    if cached is None:
        cached = [(1 << (n - d)) - 1 for d in range(1, n)]
        _MASK_CACHE[n] = cached
    return cached


def cyclic_shift_bits(bits, n, s):
    s %= n
    if s == 0:
        return bits
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    return ((bits << s) & full) | (bits >> (n - s))


def bit_max_count(bits, n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 0
    masks = get_masks(n)
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    inv = full ^ bits
    maxc = 0
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, 1):
        sh = bits >> d
        c1 = (sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
        if c1 > maxc:
            maxc = c1
        c2 = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
        if c2 > maxc:
            maxc = c2
    return maxc


def bit_counts(bits, n):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int32), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int32)
    masks = get_masks(n)
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    inv = full ^ bits
    pos = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    neg = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    for d, mask in enumerate(masks, 1):
        sh = bits >> d
        pos[d - 1] = (sh & inv & mask).bit_count()
        neg[d - 1] = (bits & (full ^ sh) & mask).bit_count()
    return pos, neg


def fix_bit_count(bits, n, m, rng):
    cnt = bits.bit_count()
    tries = 0
    while cnt != m and tries < max(5000, 100 * n):
        r = int(rng.randint(n))
        if cnt > m:
            if (bits >> r) & 1:
                bits ^= (1 << r)
                cnt -= 1
        else:
            if not ((bits >> r) & 1):
                bits |= (1 << r)
                cnt += 1
        tries += 1
    return bits


def thue_morse_scores(n):
    scores = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        v = i
        p = 0
        while v:
            p ^= (v & 1)
            v >>= 1
        scores[i] = float(p)
    return scores


def golden_scores(n):
    gr = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    frac = (x * gr) % 1.0
    return -np.abs(frac - 0.5)


def bits_from_scores(n, scores, rng):
    m = n // 2
    sc = np.asarray(scores, dtype=float).copy()
    sc += 1e-9 * rng.random_sample(n)
    idx = np.argsort(sc)[::-1][:m]
    arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if m > 0:
        arr[idx] = 1
    return array_to_bits(arr)


def choose_best_initial_bits(n, hstart, rng):
    m = n // 2
    candidates = []
    candidates.append(bits_from_scores(n, hstart, rng))
    candidates.append(bits_from_scores(n, thue_morse_scores(n), rng))
    candidates.append(bits_from_scores(n, golden_scores(n), rng))
    candidates.append(bits_from_scores(n, rng.random_sample(n), rng))

    best_bits = None
    best_c = 10 ** 9
    for b in candidates:
        b = fix_bit_count(b, n, m, rng)
        c = bit_max_count(b, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_bits = b
    if best_bits is None:
        best_bits = candidates[0]
        best_c = bit_max_count(best_bits, n)

    full = (1 << n) - 1
    shifts = [0]
    if n <= 200:
        shifts.extend(range(1, n, max(1, n // 32)))
    else:
        shifts.extend([int(s) for s in np.unique(np.linspace(1, n - 1, 24, dtype=int))])
    for s in shifts:
        b2 = cyclic_shift_bits(best_bits, n, s)
        c2 = bit_max_count(b2, n)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_bits = b2
    return best_bits


def build_bit_structs(bits, n):
    ones = []
    zeros = []
    pos1 = [-1] * n
    pos0 = [-1] * n
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.uint8)
    for i in range(n):
        if (bits >> i) & 1:
            pos1[i] = len(ones)
            ones.append(i)
            h[i] = 1
        else:
            pos0[i] = len(zeros)
            zeros.append(i)
    return ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h


def apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j):
    if i == j:
        return bits
    bits = bits ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
    if h_arr[i] == 1:
        pi = pos1[i]
        pj = pos0[j]
        if pi >= 0 and pj >= 0:
            ones[pi] = j
            pos1[j] = pi
            pos0[j] = -1
            zeros[pj] = i
            pos0[i] = pj
            pos1[i] = -1
            h_arr[i] = 0
            h_arr[j] = 1
    else:
        pi = pos0[i]
        pj = pos1[j]
        if pi >= 0 and pj >= 0:
            zeros[pi] = j
            pos0[j] = pi
            pos1[j] = -1
            ones[pj] = i
            pos1[i] = pj
            pos0[i] = -1
            h_arr[i] = 1
            h_arr[j] = 0
    return bits


def binary_local_search(bits, n, deadline, rng):
    m = n // 2
    bits = fix_bit_count(int(bits), n, m, rng)

    try_shift = int(bits)
    try_best = try_shift
    try_c = bit_max_count(try_best, n)
    if n <= 220:
        nshifts = min(n, 40)
        if nshifts > 1:
            shs = np.unique(np.linspace(1, n - 1, nshifts, dtype=int))
        else:
            shs = []
        for s in shs:
            s = int(s)
            b2 = cyclic_shift_bits(try_shift, n, s)
            c2 = bit_max_count(b2, n)
            if c2 < try_c:
                try_c = c2
                try_best = b2
    bits = try_best

    ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr = build_bit_structs(bits, n)
    best_bits = bits
    best_c = try_c
    current_c = try_c
    full = (1 << n) - 1
    masks = get_masks(n)

    T = 2.0
    swaps_since_best = 0

    while time.time() < deadline - 2.0 and remaining_time() > 2.0:
        if current_c - best_c > 8 or swaps_since_best > 3000:
            bits = best_bits
            ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr = build_bit_structs(bits, n)
            current_c = best_c
            T = 2.0
            swaps_since_best = 0

        pos_arr, neg_arr = bit_counts(bits, n)
        if pos_arr.size == 0:
            break
        vals = np.maximum(pos_arr, neg_arr)
        current_c = int(np.max(vals))
        if current_c < best_c:
            best_c = current_c
            best_bits = bits
            swaps_since_best = 0

        improved = False
        if vals.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
            inv = full ^ bits
            limit = min(40, len(order))
            for ii in order[:limit]:
                ii = int(ii)
                d = ii + 1
                if vals[ii] < max(2.0, float(current_c) - 2.0):
                    break

                if pos_arr[ii] > 2:
                    mask = masks[ii]
                    sh = bits >> d
                    pattern = sh & inv & mask
                    tries = 0
                    while pattern and tries < 40:
                        lsb = pattern & -pattern
                        i = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                        pattern ^= lsb
                        tries += 1
                        j = i + d
                        newbits = bits ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
                        newc = bit_max_count(newbits, n)
                        if newc < current_c or (newc == current_c and rng.random_sample() < 0.03):
                            bits = apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j)
                            current_c = newc
                            if newc < best_c:
                                best_c = newc
                                best_bits = bits
                            improved = True
                            swaps_since_best = 0
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break

                if not improved and neg_arr[ii] > 2:
                    mask = masks[ii]
                    sh = bits >> d
                    pattern = bits & (full ^ sh) & mask
                    tries = 0
                    while pattern and tries < 40:
                        lsb = pattern & -pattern
                        i = lsb.bit_length() - 1
                        pattern ^= lsb
                        tries += 1
                        j = i + d
                        newbits = bits ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
                        newc = bit_max_count(newbits, n)
                        if newc < current_c or (newc == current_c and rng.random_sample() < 0.03):
                            bits = apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j)
                            current_c = newc
                            if newc < best_c:
                                best_c = newc
                                best_bits = bits
                            improved = True
                            swaps_since_best = 0
                            break

        if improved:
            continue

        for _ in range(350):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 2.0:
                break
            if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
                break
            oi = int(rng.randint(len(ones)))
            zi = int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))
            i = ones[oi]
            j = zeros[zi]
            newbits = bits ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            newc = bit_max_count(newbits, n)
            delta = newc - current_c
            accept = delta < 0
            if not accept and T > 0.01 and delta <= 6:
                if rng.random_sample() < math.exp(-float(delta) / T):
                    accept = True
            if accept:
                bits = apply_swap(bits, ones, zeros, pos1, pos0, h_arr, i, j)
                current_c = newc
                swaps_since_best += 1
                if newc < best_c:
                    best_c = newc
                    best_bits = bits
                    swaps_since_best = 0
            T = max(0.05, T * 0.995)

        T = max(0.05, T * 0.97)

    return best_bits, int(best_c)


def pair_candidate_ts(hi, hj):
    tmin = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
    tmax = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
    if tmax - tmin < 1e-8:
        return []
    span = tmax - tmin
    cands = [tmin, tmax, tmin + 0.5 * span]
    for f in (0.25, 0.4, 0.6, 0.75):
        cands.append(tmin + f * span)
    t = 0.5 - hi
    if tmin < t < tmax:
        cands.append(t)
    t = hj - 0.5
    if tmin < t < tmax:
        cands.append(t)
    t = 0.5 * (hj - hi)
    if tmin < t < tmax:
        cands.append(t)

    uniq = []
    for t in cands:
        if not (tmin - 1e-9 <= t <= tmax + 1e-9):
            continue
        tc = min(max(t, tmin), tmax)
        dup = False
        for u in uniq:
            if abs(u - tc) < 1e-6:
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            uniq.append(tc)
    return uniq


def pair_local_search(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_feasible(h0, target)
    cur = c5_of(h)
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    top_list = None
    block_bad = 0
    target = 0.5 * n

    while time.time() < deadline - 3.0 and remaining_time() > 3.0:
        if top_list is None:
            corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            k = min(40, len(corr))
            top_list = [int(x) for x in np.argsort(corr)[-k:]]

        block_improved = False
        for _ in range(70):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
                break
            if top_list:
                idx = top_list[int(rng.randint(len(top_list)))]
                d = idx - (n - 1)
                if d >= 0:
                    L = n - d
                    if L <= 0:
                        continue
                    i = int(rng.randint(L))
                    j = i + d
                else:
                    s = -d
                    L = n - s
                    if L <= 0:
                        continue
                    i = int(rng.randint(L))
                    j = i + s
            else:
                i = int(rng.randint(n))
                j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue

            hi0 = h[i]
            hj0 = h[j]
            cands = pair_candidate_ts(hi0, hj0)
            improved_pair = False
            for t in cands:
                if abs(t) < 1e-7:
                    continue
                hi = hi0 + t
                hj = hj0 - t
                if hi < -1e-9 or hi > 1.0 + 1e-9 or hj < -1e-9 or hj > 1.0 + 1e-9:
                    continue
                h[i] = hi
                h[j] = hj
                c = c5_of(h)
                if c < cur - 1e-12:
                    cur = c
                    improved_pair = True
                    break
                h[i] = hi0
                h[j] = hj0

            if improved_pair:
                if cur < best:
                    best = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                block_improved = True
                top_list = None
                break
            else:
                h[i] = hi0
                h[j] = hj0

        if block_improved:
            h = project_feasible(h, target)
            cur = c5_of(h)
            if cur < best:
                best = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            block_bad = 0
        else:
            block_bad += 1
            if block_bad >= 3:
                mask = (rng.random_sample(n) < 0.18)
                h = project_feasible(h + 0.12 * rng.normal(size=n) * mask, target)
                cur = c5_of(h)
                if cur > best + 0.02:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    cur = best
                top_list = None
                block_bad = 0

    return project_feasible(best_h, target), float(best)


def softmax_smooth(q, tau):
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(qmax):
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        if w.size:
            w[0] = 1.0
        return qmax, w, qmax
    z = (q - qmax) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(q)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return qmax, w, qmax
    w = e / s
    return qmax, w, qmax + tau * math.log(s)


def weighted_gradient(weights, h, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    nz = np.nonzero(weights > 1e-10)[0]
    for idx in nz:
        idx = int(idx)
        w = float(weights[idx])
        d = idx - center
        if d >= 0:
            L = n - d
            if L <= 0:
                continue
            g[:L] -= h[d:d + L]
            g[d:d + L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
        else:
            s = -d
            L = n - s
            if L <= 0:
                continue
            g[:L] += 1.0 - h[s:s + L]
            g[s:s + L] -= h[:L]
    return g * (2.0 / n)


def projected_gradient_polish(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    target = 0.5 * n
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = project_feasible(h0, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_of(h)
    cur_c = best_c

    taus = [0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.004, 0.0015, 0.0006]
    for tau in taus:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 5.0 or remaining_time() < 5.0:
            break
        h_cur = h.copy()
        q = None
        noimp = 0
        for _ in range(500):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 5.0 or remaining_time() < 4.0:
                break
            if q is None:
                corr = np.correlate(h_cur, 1.0 - h_cur, mode="full")
                q = corr * dx
            qmax, w, cur_sc = softmax_smooth(q, tau)
            if not math.isfinite(qmax) or not math.isfinite(cur_sc):
                break
            grad = weighted_gradient(w, h_cur, n)
            gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
            if gnorm < 1e-12:
                break

            tolc = 1e-4 if tau < 0.002 else 5e-4
            scale = 1.0 / (1.0 + gnorm / 20.0)
            best_trial = None
            best_qt = None
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_mt = qmax
            best_trial_sc = cur_sc

            for b in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
                step = b * scale
                if step < 1e-7:
                    continue
                trial = project_feasible(h_cur - step * grad, target)
                corr_t = np.correlate(trial, 1.0 - trial, mode="full")
                qt = corr_t * dx
                qtmax, _, trial_sc = softmax_smooth(qt, tau)
                if not math.isfinite(trial_sc) or not math.isfinite(qtmax):
                    continue
                if trial_sc < cur_sc - 1e-13 and qtmax <= qmax + tolc:
                    if best_trial is None or trial_sc < best_trial_sc - 1e-13 or \
                       (abs(trial_sc - best_trial_sc) <= 1e-13 and qtmax < best_mt):
                        best_trial = trial
                        best_qt = qt
                        best_trial_sc = trial_sc
                        best_mt = qtmax

            if best_trial is None:
                for b in (0.005, 0.001, 0.0005):
                    step = b * scale
                    if step < 1e-8:
                        continue
                    trial = project_feasible(h_cur - step * grad, target)
                    corr_t = np.correlate(trial, 1.0 - trial, mode="full")
                    qt = corr_t * dx
                    qtmax, _, trial_sc = softmax_smooth(qt, tau)
                    if not math.isfinite(trial_sc) or not math.isfinite(qtmax):
                        continue
                    if trial_sc < cur_sc - 1e-13 and qtmax <= qmax + tolc:
                        best_trial = trial
                        best_qt = qt
                        best_trial_sc = trial_sc
                        best_mt = qtmax
                        break

            if best_trial is not None:
                h_cur = best_trial
                q = best_qt
                if best_mt < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = float(best_mt)
                    best_h = best_trial.copy()
                noimp = 0
            else:
                noimp += 1
                if noimp > 35:
                    break

        if best_c < cur_c or best_c < c5_of(h_cur):
            h = best_h.copy()
            cur_c = best_c
        else:
            h = h_cur.copy()
            cur_c = c5_of(h_cur)

    return project_feasible(best_h, target), float(best_c)


def slsqp_obj(x):
    n = _OPT_N
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    q = corr * dx
    qmax, _, sc = softmax_smooth(q, _OPT_TAU)
    if not math.isfinite(qmax) or not math.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e6
    return float(sc)


def slsqp_grad(x):
    n = _OPT_N
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    q = corr * dx
    qmax, w, _ = softmax_smooth(q, _OPT_TAU)
    g = weighted_gradient(w, x, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(n)
    return g


def slsqp_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(_OPT_N))


def slsqp_polish(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_TAU
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_feasible(h0, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_of(h)
    _OPT_N = n

    if n <= 180:
        taus = [0.03, 0.01, 0.004, 0.0015]
        maxiter_base = 250
    elif n <= 240:
        taus = [0.03, 0.01, 0.004]
        maxiter_base = 160
    else:
        taus = [0.03, 0.012]
        maxiter_base = 90

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": slsqp_con}]

    for tau in taus:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 8.0 or remaining_time() < 8.0:
            break
        _OPT_TAU = tau
        try:
            res = minimize(
                slsqp_obj,
                h,
                jac=slsqp_grad,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter_base, "ftol": 1e-12, "iprint": 0}
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            continue
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            continue
        h2 = project_feasible(x, target)
        c2 = c5_of(h2)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2

    return project_feasible(best_h, target), float(best_c)


def continuous_polish(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_feasible(h0, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_of(h)
    t_end = min(deadline, _START_TIME + _BUDGET_S - 4.0)

    if local_time_left(t_end) > 5.0:
        limit_shifts = min(n, 60 if n <= 180 else 24)
        sh, mc = try_cyclic_shifts(h, n, limit_shifts)
        c_sh = mc * (2.0 / n)
        if c_sh < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_sh
            best_h = sh.copy()
            h = sh.copy()

        sy = project_feasible(symmetrize(h), target)
        c_sy = c5_of(sy)
        if c_sy < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_sy
            best_h = sy.copy()
            h = sy.copy()

    total = max(1.0, t_end - time.time())
    if total > 70.0:
        pg_end = time.time() + 0.42 * total
        pg_end = min(pg_end, t_end - 35.0)
        if time.time() < pg_end and remaining_time() > 10.0:
            h2, c2 = projected_gradient_polish(h, n, pg_end, rng)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()

    if n <= 240 and time.time() < t_end - 35.0 and remaining_time() > 30.0:
        sl_end = time.time() + 0.22 * total
        sl_end = min(sl_end, t_end - 15.0)
        if time.time() < sl_end:
            h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(h, n, sl_end, rng)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()

    if time.time() < t_end - 8.0 and remaining_time() > 10.0:
        pair_end = min(t_end - 3.0, time.time() + max(8.0, 0.45 * (t_end - time.time())))
        h2, c2 = pair_local_search(h, n, pair_end, rng)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()

    if time.time() < t_end - 25.0 and remaining_time() > 25.0:
        if n <= 200:
            h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(h, n, t_end - 4.0, rng)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()
        else:
            h2, c2 = pair_local_search(h, n, t_end - 4.0, rng)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
                h = h2.copy()

    return project_feasible(best_h, target), float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _START_TIME, _BUDGET_S, _OPT_N, _OPT_TAU
    _START_TIME = time.time()
    _BUDGET_S = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    global_deadline = _START_TIME + _BUDGET_S - 8.0

    parent_h = None
    try:
        pv = initial_h_values
        if pv is not None:
            ph = np.asarray(pv, dtype=float)
            if ph.size > 0:
                parent_h = ph
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    if parent_h is not None:
        best_n = len(parent_h)
        best_h = project_feasible(parent_h, 0.5 * best_n)
        best_c5 = c5_of(best_h)
    else:
        best_n = 160
        best_h = None
        best_c5 = 1e100
        seeds = []
        for freq, phase in ((1, 0.0), (1, math.pi / 4), (2, 0.0), (2, math.pi / 3), (3, 0.0)):
            seeds.append(make_sine(best_n, freq, phase, 0.5))
        seeds.append(make_random_h(best_n, rng))
        bits_tm = bits_from_scores(best_n, thue_morse_scores(best_n), rng)
        seeds.append(bits_to_array(bits_tm, best_n))
        for hs in seeds:
            hs = project_feasible(hs, 0.5 * best_n)
            c = c5_of(hs)
            if c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c
                best_h = hs.copy()
        if best_h is None:
            best_h = make_sine(best_n, 1, 0.0, 0.5)
            best_c5 = c5_of(best_h)

    if remaining_time() < 5:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
        best_c5 = c5_of(best_h)
        return (best_h, best_c5, best_n)

    sh, mcsh = try_cyclic_shifts(best_h, best_n, min(best_n, 96))
    csh = mcsh * (2.0 / best_n)
    if csh < best_c5 - 1e-14:
        best_c5 = csh
        best_h = sh.copy()

    sy = project_feasible(symmetrize(best_h), 0.5 * best_n)
    csy = c5_of(sy)
    if csy < best_c5 - 1e-14:
        best_c5 = csy
        best_h = sy.copy()

    if time.time() < _START_TIME + 8.0 and remaining_time() > 10.0:
        for freq, phase in ((1, 0.0), (1, math.pi / 2), (1, math.pi),
                            (2, 0.0), (2, math.pi / 4), (3, 0.0), (3, math.pi / 3)):
            hs = make_sine(best_n, freq, phase, 0.5)
            hs2, mc2 = try_cyclic_shifts(hs, best_n, min(best_n, 36))
            c2 = mc2 * (2.0 / best_n)
            if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
                best_c5 = c2
                best_h = hs2.copy()

    if remaining_time() > 70.0:
        alloc = min(360.0, remaining_time() * 0.30)
        dl = min(global_deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h2, c2 = continuous_polish(best_h, best_n, dl, rng)
        if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
            best_c5 = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    bin_budget = min(450.0, remaining_time() * 0.35)
    if bin_budget > 50.0:
        bin_pref = [best_n]
        for x in (128, 96, 160, 192, 224, 64, 112, 144, 176, 208, 240, 256, 288, 320):
            bin_pref.append(x)
        seen = set()
        bin_list = []
        for x in bin_pref:
            x = int(x)
            if x % 2 == 0 and 32 <= x <= 320 and x not in seen:
                seen.add(x)
                bin_list.append(x)

        for idx, n in enumerate(bin_list):
            if remaining_time() < 45.0 or time.time() >= global_deadline:
                break
            if idx == 0:
                alloc = min(120.0, bin_budget * 0.32)
            elif idx == 1:
                alloc = min(90.0, bin_budget * 0.22)
            else:
                alloc = max(25.0, bin_budget * 0.035)
            alloc = min(alloc, remaining_time() - 35.0)
            if alloc < 20.0:
                break
            dl = min(global_deadline, time.time() + alloc)

            if n == best_n:
                hstart = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).copy()
            else:
                hstart = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n)
            hstart = project_feasible(hstart, 0.5 * n)
            init_bits = choose_best_initial_bits(n, hstart, rng)
            bits, cnt = binary_local_search(init_bits, n, dl, rng)
            arr = bits_to_array(bits, n)
            arr = project_feasible(arr, 0.5 * n)
            c2 = c5_of(arr)
            if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
                best_c5 = c2
                best_h = arr.copy()
                best_n = n

    cont_pref = [best_n]
    for x in (144, 176, 192, 224, 256, 200, 288, 320, 384):
        cont_pref.append(x)
    seen = set()
    cont_list = []
    for x in cont_pref:
        x = int(x)
        if 64 <= x <= 400 and x not in seen:
            seen.add(x)
            cont_list.append(x)

    for n in cont_list:
        if remaining_time() < 80.0 or time.time() >= global_deadline:
            break
        alloc = min(140.0, remaining_time() * 0.13)
        alloc = min(alloc, remaining_time() - 30.0)
        if alloc < 30.0:
            break
        dl = min(global_deadline, time.time() + alloc)

        if n == best_n:
            hstart = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).copy()
        else:
            hstart = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n)
        hstart = project_feasible(hstart, 0.5 * n)
        h2, c2 = continuous_polish(hstart, n, dl, rng)
        if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
            best_c5 = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            best_n = n

    if remaining_time() > 35.0:
        alloc = min(200.0, remaining_time() * 0.45)
        dl = min(global_deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        h2, c2 = continuous_polish(best_h, best_n, dl, rng)
        if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
            best_c5 = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    if remaining_time() > 25.0 and best_n % 2 == 0:
        dl = min(global_deadline, time.time() + min(25.0, remaining_time() - 10.0))
        if dl > time.time() + 8.0:
            init_bits = choose_best_initial_bits(best_n, best_h, rng)
            bits, cnt = binary_local_search(init_bits, best_n, dl, rng)
            arr = bits_to_array(bits, best_n)
            arr = project_feasible(arr, 0.5 * best_n)
            c2 = c5_of(arr)
            if c2 < best_c5 - 1e-14:
                best_c5 = c2
                best_h = arr.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    final_c5 = c5_of(best_h)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ev is not None and math.isfinite(float(ev)) and float(ev) < final_c5:
            final_c5 = float(ev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
